**Marketplace design (2026-10-24)** (game-designer, inside Tyler's 10-24 objective: "as convenient as a modern trading application while retaining the atmosphere of a classic fantasy MMORPG" and "no duping or exploiting can occur"; lane/d-market-design, design only, no code). Builds on the live market-v2 core (`2026-08-17-market-v2.sql`: escrow out of the bag, seller paid at sale time, immutable listing columns, delete-as-arbiter, idempotent intents; `hr_market_buy` on the account purse since `2026-12-15-account-gold.sql`). Production, read-only on 10-23: 0 listings, 0 sales, 2 accounts, 8 heroes, purses 182,656 and 452,942 gold.

**0. Stacks, not single pieces (the answer first).** Every copy of an item id is identical, so a listing is an item id and a count and a buyer can take any part of it. Evidence: the bag is `player_inventory (user_id, slot, item_id) → qty` (`2026-08-11-player-state.sql`), worn gear is `player_equipment (user_id, slot, equip_slot) → item_id` (equipping debits one unit), Storage is `account_bank (user_id, item_id) → qty` (`2026-11-27-account-depot.sql`), a hunt's packed supplies are `hunt_bag (session_id, item_id) → qty` (`2026-10-21-hunt-sessions.sql`), and a listing is `market_listings.item_id + qty`. No table has a wear, durability, roll or per-piece column. Gear stats are fixed (Tyler 10-14). Imbuing is not built (`src/data/imbuements.js`: "no server table"). The one upgrade that exists, the weapon enchant, is `player_state.enchant {equip_slot: element}`: it sits on the hero's worn slot, not on the item. **Rule for later lanes:** any per-piece state (the L4 imbuement) lives on the worn slot, and an imbued piece cannot be taken off until its imbuement runs out or is removed. That is Tyler's 10-15 "untradable until expiry or paid removal", and it means a stack never holds a copy that differs from the rest.

**1. Purpose test.**
- **Problem it solves.** Surplus has one outlet today: the shop. On 10-23, one account's four heroes held 59 Iron Platelegs in their bags (28, 15, 10 and 6); the shop pays 142 each (0.5 of book 285; tier-3 gear gets 0.14). Gear drops about once an hour per slot per weight (Tyler 10-23), so most of it is surplus to the hero who finds it and an upgrade for someone else. Materials move more volume. By book value the biggest holdings are Normal Logs (22,995), Steel Whetstones (1,340), Raw Silverfin, Cooked Shrimp (4,472), Raw Shrimp, Coal and Oak Planks. Example: the shop pays 9 for a Cooked Shrimp (heals 96). A hunter without one pays 14 per use for the store's Salted Shrimp (heals 72), so any price from 10 to 18 leaves both players better off. At 13 the cook gets 12.48 after the cut (+39% over the shop), and the hunter pays 0.135 g per HP instead of the store's 0.194 (-30%).
- **Decisions it adds.** At every drop: keep it, store it, sell it at the shop, or list it (and at what price). Farm or buy: an hour of Mosslight for the platelegs, or 240 gold now. Which ground to farm, because the market value of a ground's drops becomes another axis grounds compete on (DESIGN_STANDARDS §5). Whether to craft for sale.
- **What it plugs into.** Loot bands and loot rules, Storage (per account), the account purse, crafting inputs and outputs, and housing costs: the War Crown is Chief Coppercap's rare (11 found so far), and 4 of them are part of the Shrine's last rung, The Ossuary; planks and bars build the storage chests. Also hunt supplies, the Bestiary (where each item drops) and gold history (10-23).
- **Gold sink.** A 1% listing fee plus the Weighhouse's 4% cut of each sale, both destroyed. Over the last 14 days about 785k gold came in and about 150k went out (unverified split: 512k of the inflow predates `gold_kind` stamping). Market gold is a transfer, never minted, so the sink grows with trade and leaves the loot model's mint caps alone.
- **Hour 100 and hour 1,000.** Yes: Trophies (~140 h), War Crowns, next-tier gear in every zone, housing materials. It is depth rather than extra complexity: one window with four tabs.

**2. What the player sees.**
- **The place.** The Weighhouse on the Common's square, beside the well, kept by the weighmaster Aldous Pell. Both names were checked: unused in the repo and free of banned words. A weighhouse is the real town building where goods were weighed for trade. The fiction explains the rules: the Weighhouse holds what you list, pays your purse the moment it sells, and carries unsold goods back to your storage. Tapping the building or Aldous opens the Market (new `TAP_OPENS` rows plus a map object in `town_common.js`).
- **How far convenience reaches: everywhere.** The icon row has a Market button, and the market works fully for every hero, hunting or not. It is a window on desktop and a slide-out sheet on landscape phones. Why: heroes hunt for hours on the server, and the market moves account goods (Storage, the purse) that no single hero carries. Walking to the same building on every visit repeats a decision already made, which breaks the first principle. The atmosphere comes from the place, the players seen there in town, the weighmaster's line and the ledger look, not from a walk. *Handoff:* the Storage window's "Leave the hunt to use it" is client-only (`hr_bank_move` has no hunt check) and conflicts with this and with Quick stash (10-23). Systems and the Art Director should revisit it.
- **Browse.** One row per ITEM, not per listing: icon, name, tier and weight, "from 13 each", "320 for sale", "last sold 13, 2 h ago". The categories are the bag's (`bag-model.js` BAG_CATS: All, Gear, Food, Drinks, Ammo & runes, Loot, Materials, Rare, Other), with Gear split by slot and weight. Search covers every tradeable item, whether listed or not ("No one is selling Coal. Last sold 2 days ago for 12 each."). Filters: Usable by <hero> (meets the requirement), armour weight, slot, tier, For sale now. Sort: lowest price (the default), most for sale, recently sold, name. The account remembers all of these.
- **Item page.** What the item is for and where it drops or how it is made (the Bestiary and recipe rows). "For sale" grouped by price ("13 each · 180", "14 each · 60"), a 30-day line of the daily median price with volume, and the last 10 sales (count, price each, time ago). Your own listings show "Yours" with Cancel.
- **Price discovery.** Every row shows the lowest ask, how many are for sale, the last sale and the 7-day median. The bag, Storage and loot-rule tooltips gain one line under "The shop pays 9 each" (`item-tip.js`): "Market: from 13 · last sold 13". History stays honest because same-account buys are refused and same-network sales are left out of it (§3, §4).
- **Buying.** Pick a quantity (1, 10, 100, Most I can afford, All) and it fills cheapest first across listings: "Buy 120 for 1,560 gold". The delivery choice, "Goes to: Storage" (default) or "Goes to: <hero>'s bag", is remembered. The sheet shows the purse after the purchase and warns if a running hunt's "Keep at least" gold floor would be crossed. One press buys. In v1 the client buys the listings in the confirmation one by one. If one sells first it stops and says so ("Bought 100. The last 20 at 13 sold first. Buy 20 at 14?"), and it never pays above the confirmed price; listing prices cannot change after posting.
- **Selling.** Items can be listed from the bag or from Storage: "List on the market…" in their menus, or the Sell tab, which shows the viewed hero's bag and Storage with "Market: from X" and "The shop pays Y" on each row. The sell sheet has a quantity (All, half, custom) and a price each, with chips: Lowest now, Last sold, Your last price, and Suggested (the catalogue value, used when there is no market data). Before pressing, the player sees: "Listing fee: 8 gold now (kept if it doesn't sell). When one sells, the Weighhouse keeps 4%: you get 230 each. Listed for 7 days; anything unsold comes back to your storage."
- **Buy orders in v1: no.** A buy order holds gold out of the purse, and running hunts spend from that purse every time they use a store supply (store bills, the gold_out end, the "Keep at least" floor). That needs its own rule ("held for buy orders"; hunts spend only the free part of the purse) and its own matching surface, so it is a later lane.
- **Length, cancel, expiry.** One length, 7 days; the fee doesn't depend on it, so there is no choice to make. Cancel any time: the goods go to Storage at once and the fee is kept. At expiry the goods go back to Storage automatically.
- **Sale notices.** Online: a toast and a System chat line per sale, batched per minute, following the `alerts.js` pattern used for Tamsin's tally payments. Away: one line in the away recap. Gold history lists each sale (kind `trade`).
- **My listings and History.** My listings shows each listing as "2 of 3 left · 240 each · 6 days left · received 230" with Cancel. History covers 30 days of sales, purchases, returns and fees, with "List again" on anything that came back, "List all again" (same prices) and "Buy again". These, the remembered price chip and the remembered delivery choice cover every decision a seller or buyer would otherwise repeat.
- **Anonymous.** No seller or buyer name appears anywhere in the market. It is an exchange, not a stall: names invite harassment, price griefing and real-money-trade coordination. Named, social trading is right-click Trade, its own later lane.

| Where | Words (plain English, copy-lint) |
|---|---|
| Icon row / window | "Market" / "The Weighhouse". Tabs: Browse · Sell · My listings · History |
| Greeting (top line) | "Aldous Pell, weighmaster: I hold what you list and pay you the moment it sells." |
| Buy | "Buy 120 for 1,560 gold" · "Goes to: Storage" / "Goes to: Brannoc's bag" · "You'll have 181,096 gold left." · "Brannoc's hunt stops buying food below 2,000 gold. This leaves 1,500." |
| Sell | "List on the market…" · "List 3 for 240 each" · "Brannoc's setup Sewer tank wears this. List it anyway?" |
| Cancel / return | "Cancelled. 2 Iron Platelegs are back in your storage." · "2 Iron Platelegs didn't sell and are back in your storage." |
| Sale | "The Weighhouse sold 1 Iron Platelegs for 240 gold. You received 230." · Away recap: "While you were away the Weighhouse sold 4 lots for 1,727 gold." |
| Storage window | "73 of 100 stacks. 2 more are kept free for your market listings." |

| Refusal (server code) | What the player reads |
|---|---|
| `not_tradeable` / `no_value` | "Starter kit, gifts and trophies can't be sold to other players." |
| `item_locked` | "This is locked. Unlock it in your bag to list it." |
| `insufficient_item` | "You don't have that many any more." |
| `price_below_shop` | "The shop pays 142 for one. Ask at least that, or sell it at the shop." |
| `price_above_cap` | "That's more than this can be listed for. The most is 28,500 each." |
| `storage_no_room` | "Unsold items come back to your storage, and it has no room for a new kind. Make room, or build the Millrace Crate in the House." |
| `too_many_listings` / `list_daily_cap` | "You have 50 listings up. Cancel one or wait for a sale." / "That's 200 listings today. You can list again after the daily reset at <local time>." |
| `insufficient_gold` | "The listing fee is 8 gold." / "You need 1,560 gold. You have 1,200." |
| `gone` / `not_enough` / `expired` | "Someone bought those first. The next cheapest is 14 each." / "Only 2 are left at that price." / "That listing just ran out." |
| `own_listing` | "That's your own listing. Cancel it in My listings to take it back." |
| `bag_full` / `storage_full` | "Brannoc's bag is full. Send it to your storage instead." / "Your storage is full. Send it to a hero's bag, or make room." |
| `spend_daily_cap` / `proceeds_daily_cap` | "That's the most the market lets you spend in one day. Try again after the daily reset." / "This listing can't be bought right now. Try the next one." |
| `rate_limited` | "Too fast. Wait a moment and try again." (`version_conflict` and `settle_first` are retried quietly and never shown) |

**3. Rules and numbers** (every number is an `hr_market_config` value, PROVISIONAL, and gets re-tuned with Tyler's economy sims; the per-unit rules hold for any number).

| Rule | Value | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Who owns a listing | the ACCOUNT; the listing hero is recorded for "listed by" in My listings only | gold is per account (10-23) and unsold goods return to the account's Storage; any hero of the account can cancel |
| Sources | the viewed hero's bag, or Storage | Tyler's brief; both are server rows the market debits under their own lock |
| Returns (cancel, expiry, item made unlistable) | always into Storage, never refused | one destination, and it never fills a hunting hero's bag |
| Storage room | room used = kinds in Storage + kinds the account has listed (one function, read by `hr_bank_move` and the market) | otherwise listing 1 unit at 1 gold of fee and letting it return would open Storage stacks past capacity, skipping the storage chests (a 1,500 to 90,000 g sink). Returns always fit, and nothing ever goes over the cap |
| Delivery | Storage (default) or the buying hero's bag; refused if full | the buyer is present and can choose again |
| Listing fee | 1% of qty x ask, rounded up, at least 1; paid at listing, kept on cancel and expiry | a sink; makes cancel-and-relist undercut wars cost something; no refund path to exploit |
| Weighhouse cut | 4% of each sale, rounded up (`house_tax_bp` 150 → 400), out of the seller's proceeds | the sink. 5% in all is low enough that listing beats arranging a trade off the market (judgement, unverified) |
| Price floor | at least the shop's bid for one (`hr_hunt_vendor_value.unit`: all 508 listable items have one, and none is above book) | no listing asks less than the shop pays, so buying a listing to sell it to the shop can never profit, and a "gift" listing can move at most book minus the shop's bid (zero for a War Crown, whose bid is its book) |
| Price ceiling | at most 100 x book value per unit, never under 1,000 | stops absurd asks and fat fingers; the top-book item (270,000) can still ask 27M; makes laundering through junk need bulk volume |
| Open listings | 50 per account | covers a four-hero account's surplus |
| New listings | 200 per account per UTC day | the old per-slot churn cap, moved to the account |
| Length | 7 days, one length | an idle player away for days still sells |
| Daily caps | 2,000,000 spent and 2,000,000 received per account per UTC day | 2 to 4.5 days of a four-hero account hunting around the clock at Mosslight's measured rate (coins 4.6k per hero-hour; coins plus loot at book value about 10.7k; 21-day live tallies): it should not bind an honest player at EA (unverified), and it bounds real-money-trade throughput; Security may lower the fuse |
| Same account | refused (`own_listing`), whatever the heroes | Storage moves items between your heroes for free, so a self-buy is only a fake price |
| Same network (accounts sharing a hashed IP within 24 h) | allowed, flagged in the ledger, left out of price history | households share an IP; Security sees the pattern |
| Never listable | worn gear and a running hunt's packed supplies (they are not in the bag: §4 #3, #4); the 6 untradeable items (Hearthfind trophies, Dragon Relic, Void Essence); the 16 worth-nothing items (the practice kit, the Oak Practice Blade, 3 recipe books, Air Rune, Bronze Arrows, Coarse Whetstone); locked items; store basics (Salted Shrimp is bought per use and is not an item); Crowns; anything bought with Crowns or given once per account (authoring rule: such items are untradeable and worth nothing) | no faucet can turn into market gold: premium's daily weapon, new heroes' kits and gifts are worth nothing; Crowns-for-gold stays the separate, capped exchange that needs Steam and legal review |

**4. Ways an item or gold could be in two places, or appear from nothing** (a list for Security's independent review; "live" = already enforced by the market-v2 core).

| # | How it could happen | The rule that closes it |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | List the same units twice (double press, two tabs, two heroes listing from Storage) | listing DEBITS the source under its lock (bag: the hero's lock; Storage: the account depot lock); the second debit finds too few; idempotent intent keys (live for the bag) |
| 2 | A cancel, an expiry and a buy race over one listing | the listing row is the arbiter: `delete … returning` / qty decrement under the row lock, so exactly one wins each unit (live) |
| 3 | List supplies a running hunt is eating (the hunt keeps going with the browser closed) | Enter MOVES whole supply stacks into `hunt_bag` (`hr_hunt_enter_for`, `packFor`); the market reads only the bag and Storage, so packed supplies cannot be reached; restock moves bag → hunt bag under the same lock |
| 4 | List gear that is being worn | worn copies live in `player_equipment`, not the bag (equipping debits one); a saved setup that names a listed piece just fails its equip, and the sell sheet warns |
| 5 | Loot from a running hunt: list it while the hunt sells it to cover or under a loot rule | the settle takes at most what the bag holds (`min(held, …)`, `2026-12-15-account-gold.sql`) under the hero's lock (`hr_hunt_lock` = `hr_apply`'s key), the same lock the market takes |
| 6 | A retried buy (lost response) buys twice | the intent key replays the stored result; the intent name binds listing + count (live) |
| 7 | The seller is paid twice, or never | paid in the buy's own transaction with no collect step; the credit's row count is checked (live) |
| 8 | Two heroes of one account, or a hunt settle and a buy, overspend the shared purse | every gold move goes through `hr_purse_move` under the purse row lock; the purse can never go below 0; a hunt bill refused for `gold_short` re-reads (account-gold R5, R7) |
| 9 | A return lost because Storage is full, or forced over the cap | the Storage-room rule (§3): a listing keeps its return space |
| 10 | The market used as free storage | listings count against Storage room, cost a fee per week and can be bought at their price; there is no "expired, waiting" state |
| 11 | An expired listing, or one whose item became unlistable, is still bought | the buy re-checks expiry (live) and listability under the lock; the expiry sweep returns unlistable listings early |
| 12 | Price changed after a buyer confirmed | `ask_each` cannot change once posted (trigger, live); the buyer sends only listing + count |
| 13 | Rounding: fee or cut paid as 0 by splitting into 1-gold sales | both rounded up; the fee is at least 1 (cut rounding is live) |
| 14 | Overflow in qty x price | numeric arithmetic + `max_gross` 100M per listing (live) |
| 15 | Mint gold by buying cheap and selling to the shop | the floor = the shop's bid; the shop's per-hero daily gold budget still applies |
| 16 | Market prices break the loot redesign's gold caps | market gold is conserved (transfers); the only gear-to-gold mint is still the shop at 0.5/0.5/0.14 |
| 17 | Faucets turned into gold: start kit, the premium daily weapon, gifts, hero create loops | all worth nothing → unlistable; there is no hero deletion (no RPC exists), so no create-and-strip loop |
| 18 | Crowns past the weekly exchange cap | Crowns never trade here; Crown-bought goods are untradeable |
| 19 | Wash trades and fake price history | same account refused; same network flagged and left out of history |
| 20 | Real-money gold laundering via junk at high prices | floor, ceiling, fee + cut, per-account daily caps, both accounts in the ledger meta (never shown to players), sales at the ceiling or over 20x the 30-day median flagged |
| 21 | Identity leak | `market_listings` is select-all in the 08-17 file and holds `seller_user_id` and `seller_name` (production shows no client grant on 10-23): browse goes through a names-free read |
| 22 | The old client-authored buy-offer table | `market_buy_offers` (0 rows, read policy only): cut |
| 23 | Town work spends an input the player just listed | inputs are read under the hero's lock at each settle; work stops with "ran out", never crafts from a listing (ASSUMED: prove in lane 1) |
| 24 | A market verb priced against an unsettled window | `hr_settle_first_of` on every market verb (live on buy and cancel; add to list); lane 1 proves it never stalls a hero whose hunt runs as a session |

**5. Walk-through against real data** (the two live accounts on 10-23: seller A, purse 182,656, Storage 1 stack; buyer B, purse 452,942. The market rows are this design and become playable as lanes 1-4 ship).

| # | Step | Holds / can reach (steps 1..N-1 + real state) | Proof (grepped / read) |
|---|---|---|---|

A's result on the piece that sold: 230 - 8 fee = 222 gold, against 142 at the shop (+56%). B paid 240 instead of hunting about an hour of Mosslight for that slot and weight.

**6. v1 scope (the smallest market that already feels great) and the lanes, in order.**
1. **C, server (backend-architect): `2026-12-28-market-account.sql`.** Listings owned by the account (the listing hero kept as provenance; `source` bag|storage; `qty_listed`; `fee`). List from the bag (hero lock) or Storage (depot lock) with the listability rule, floor and ceiling, and the fee through `hr_purse_move`. Buy: same account refused, delivery to Storage or the bag, the 4% cut, per-account daily caps, listability re-checked. Cancel by any hero of the account; expiry and catalogue returns into Storage. The Storage-room function, with `hr_bank_move` restated to use it. Names-free reads: browse, the item page (asks grouped by price, the 30-day daily median and volume, the last sales), and own listings with 30-day history; public table reads revoked. Config values from §3. Cut `market_buy_offers`. Extend `tests/market-v2.mjs` with conservation over purse + Storage + bag + escrow, the same-account refusal, Storage room, floor and ceiling, and the fee. One lock order, written down once. The §4 self-check.
2. **Edge (same lane or systems-engineer):** `market_list`, `market_cancel`, `market_buy` and the three reads through hr-accrue's one intent transport and rate buckets, behind a server switch that stays OFF.
3. **Security:** one adversarial review of lanes 1-2 against §4, before any player sees the market (money surface; Security holds veto).
4. **B, client (systems-engineer + art-director):** the Market window and sheet (Browse, item page, Sell, My listings, History), the tooltip line, sale toasts and the away-recap line, refusal words, and "kept free for your market listings" in the Storage window. Master-detail on desktop (category rail, item list, item page); on phones a sheet that opens the item page on top. Art-director layout pass and the visual gate. Hidden until the switch.
5. **Art (asset-director, can run alongside 1-2):** the Weighhouse building (style 1, for the Common's square), Aldous Pell (town NPC), a 32 px Market icon (a balance scale), and the map row plus `TAP_OPENS` rows.

**Later:** buy orders (gold held out of the purse, hunts spend only the free part, instant sale to the best order); one server intent that buys across many listings up to a maximum price; refill from the market (the one-tap refill fills a target from listings up to a set price, so a recurring purchase is decided once); a watchlist and price alerts; an "always list at the lowest price" loot rule; right-click Trade (its own lane); the Crowns exchange (Tyler, legal).

*Done and verified (code read + read-only production queries, 10-23):* the per-piece answer; the hunt-bag and worn-gear reservations; the purse lock; the floor's coverage (508 of 508); the v:0 and untradeable lists; no hero deletion; the identity columns on the public listings policy; every item, monster, ground, price and building named above. *Not verified:* nothing is played (the market is not built); the fee, cut, caps and ceiling are not checked against an economy sim (marked provisional); §4 #23 (town work) and #24 (settle-first on hunt sessions) are for lane 1 to prove. *Assumptions:* the hashed IP the 4-per-IP cap uses can stamp same-network sales; the account wipe at EA clears the market with everything else.

**Market window, as built (2026-10-31, lane/b-market-window, systems-engineer).** Lane 4 of the design above. Calls the design left open, each provisional: (1) threat model #9 is `market_locked` on list and buy below `hr_market_config.min_level`, default any hero at Level 10, the threat model's own example; designer to re-tune with the sims. Cancel and reads stay open. (2) Market review change 5: the Sell sheet's one-press chips are Suggested (the catalogue) and Your last price; Lowest now and Last sold stand beside the box as numbers with their sales count until the read carries #10's sample rules. (3) The icon shows whether or not the switch is on: the window says so when it is off, and the release applies the switch before its client push. (4) Sale notices are one `hr_market_mine` read on sign-in and one a minute only while the account has a listing up and the page shows; a push on the private inbox topic can replace it. **Not built, later lanes:** List all again, the tooltip price line, Usable-by and gear slot filters, the town building and Aldous Pell on the map, buy orders. **Open:** Storage's "Leave the hunt to use it" is unchanged (design handoff). A character wipe must delete `market_listings`, `market_sales` and `market_item_daily` with the sweep paused (market review change 3): no wipe procedure exists in the repo yet.

**Security: marketplace threat model (2026-10-24)** (security-engineer, lane/s-market-threat; review only: no migration, no production write. Evidence: the last definition in apply order of every live writer of `player_inventory`, `player_equipment`, `account_bank`, `hunt_bag`, `market_listings` and `account_wallet` (45 bodies by a source scan); production's executable surface (147 functions) and table grants, read-only, 10-23 ~23:00 UTC; ten proofs replayed on the whole migration chain in PGlite (`_archive/security-proofs/market-threat-proofs.mjs`, gitignored, 10 of 10 CONFIRMED).)

**Verdict: GO-WITH-CHANGES as the basis for lane 1. The market stays BLOCKED from players until lane 3 signs off lanes 1-2 with the changes below.** The core holds: escrow is a debit under the owner's lock, the listing row is the arbiter for cancel, expiry and buy, the price comes from the locked row, gold moves only through `hr_purse_move`, and stacks are fungible, so no per-copy state can fork. No way to duplicate an item or gold was found, today or in this design.

**Today-dupes: none found.** Checked: `hr_apply` items and equip (row lock, negative stack refused, version CAS); `hr_bank_move` (hero row, then the depot lock); hunt enter, restock, close, swap and forfeit (every session end returns or moves the packed bag, so the retention prune never deletes live supplies); cover and the loot "Sell" rule (`min(held, this session's loot)` under `hr_hunt_lock`); the shared farm (hero row, then the farm lock: the second harvester sees the emptied plot; the replant pays through the farm purse); tally pay; `hr_unlock_buy` (hero lock, idempotency claim, debits `for update`); `hr_create_character` (one kit per owned slot, re-checked under its lock); companion grant (an unlock row, not an item); clan deposits (one-way sinks). The edge's `vendorPriceOf` equals `hr_hunt_vendor_value` for all 510 items, so the floor is the real NPC bid.

**The designer's open questions.**
- **§4 #23, town work cannot consume a listed input: PROVEN** (P4). A craft delta computed before the listing is refused `version_conflict`; one computed after is refused `insufficient_item`; the escrow is intact. The accrue verb and the tick fence both commit under the version CAS, and `hr_apply` refuses a negative stack under the row lock.
- **§4 #24, settle-first does not stall a hunting hero: PROVEN for hunts** (P5; production: the one running hunt's `accrued_to` was 11 s behind against the 180 s threshold, because every settle stamps it). **It does refuse a hero on offline town work:** 4 of the 5 live gatherers were 2.1 h unsettled, and `hr_settle_first_of` answers `settle_first` for them. So the bag verbs (list from the bag, buy into a bag) register `collectsFirst` in the edge, and the Storage-only verbs (list from Storage, buy into Storage, cancel, expiry) carry no settle-first at all: Storage is not an engine input.
- **Storage's "Leave the hunt to use it": the server must NOT enforce it.** A hunting hero's Storage move cannot reach packed supplies (P6: `hunt_bag` untouched; a deposit beyond the bag is `insufficient_item`), and the hunt engine reads only `hunt_bag` plus cover candidates bounded by `min(bag, this session's loot)`, so a mid-hunt move cannot duplicate or reprice anything. Remove the client gate (it is the "client refuses, server allows" class) once #7 below is fixed.

**Lane 1 must include:** #1, #2, #3, #4, #5, #6, #13 and #14, plus one Storage-room function used by every `account_bank` writer (returns from cancel and expiry are never refused) and settle-first only on the bag verbs. **Lane 2:** `collectsFirst` on the bag verbs only, the network hash (#8), the race legs in #2 and #3. **Backend hunt-settle lane, before Quick stash or market-from-bag:** #7. **Designer:** #9, #10, the kit's valued items.

**Residual risks accepted once the changes land (bounded and journalled):** gold moved between unrelated accounts (laundering, real-money trade) up to 2M a day per account at a 5% loss, both accounts recorded in the no-grant sales table; a compromised `hr-accrue` can still name any user on the engine verbs, limited to conserved transfers of listable items inside the per-account caps. **Remaining, with trigger:** #7 is live today for allowlisted parties (a member racing their own bag); same-network detection is absent until #8 lands. Side note for the Coordinator, not security: town work is not settled server-side while its hero is away (4 live gatherers were 2.1 h behind), against the 10-22 ruling that town work updates in real time.

- **2026-10-22, de-boxing the UI (art director, after Tyler's "our UI is super boxy... the action bar is dorky, no art to it"; lane/b-ui-art-pass; shots in `art/review/ui-art-pass/`).** **Why it reads boxy (measured on `before-hunt-1440x900.png`):** (a) every element is the same object: party, analyzer, hunt bar, tip, action bar and windows are all a flat `--world-hud-bg` rectangle in the same 2-art-px hairline frame (`frame-iron-thin.png`), which at 1440 reads as a 1 px grey CSS border, not iron, so nothing has more weight than anything else; (b) boxes inside boxes: the bar holds 20 identical lined squares plus a select, a segmented control, a stepper and an input; (c) flat fills with no material; (d) the bar was 970 x 182 px (20% of the screen height), and its right third is a web form; (e) the hub was smooth SVG rings, web marks on pixel stone. **Built this lane:** the bar is mounted on a forged 6-art-px iron plate (`bar-plate.png`), slots are sockets cut into it (`bar-socket.png`), the medallion is pixel art (`bar-medal.png`, `-sm` on the phone) whose top breaks the plate's edge, with the HP/MP arcs in its gauge channel and the XP gilding its rim; a groove parts the settings; 182 -> 178 px tall (`after-hunt-1440x900.png`, `after-bar-1440-dpr2.png`, `after-bar-922.png`). All five PNGs come from `tools/make-hud-bar-art.mjs` (palette colours, `--check`). **AW-17 vs the docked-window cap (CONFLICTS 2026-10-22, RULED):** a skill row now puts its XP to go and level on the name line (`windows.css .hw-skill` grid), so Gathering and crafting needs 486 px of body, not 633; above the bar at 1440x900 it gets 502 (`before/after-gathering-1440-crop.png`); the bar stays put and nothing scrolls. **The plan, ranked by impact (each is cheap unless marked):**
  1. **BUILT 2026-10-22 (same lane): the bar is 122 px tall (was 182), the settings fold out above its end from the Fight settings button (`after-foldout-hunt-1440x900*.png`).** **The fight settings leave the desktop bar** (as on the phone: the set's name, Saved setups and a fold-out panel above the bar). Removes the web form, the plate shrinks to the slots + hub (~120 px tall, ~760 wide), the medallion can rise fully above the plate. Before: `before-bar-1440-dpr2.png`; after: the phone already shows it (`after-bar-922.png`). Medium: HS-12 drives the desktop panel and must open it first.
  2. **Earned frames only:** the passive HUD widgets (party, analyzer, the hunt bar) lose the iron hairline and sit on the plate colour with a header value step; the iron frame is kept for the action bar and windows (amends ART_DIRECTION §9's "HUD widgets a 2-art-px frame"). Before: `before-hunt-1440x900.png` (five identical frames). Cheap.
  3. **Windows get the plate:** `frame-iron.png` (4 art px) becomes a riveted plate like the bar's, with a title band one value step up, so a window is an object and not a bordered div. Before: `before-win-inventory-1440x900.png`. Cheap.
  4. **Empty slots fade back:** an empty socket shows no number until hover or drag, and the second row only shows once anything is bound (WORLD_SHELL already says so). Before: 15 "+ n" cells (`before-bar-1440.png`). Cheap; HS-8 asserts the "+" shows.
  5. **The hunt bar is a nameplate:** `.ht-bar` drops its frame for a hanging place-name banner (Cinzel is allowed for place names, §10) with the three commands as quiet text under it. Before: the top-centre box in `before-hunt-1440x900.png`. Cheap.
  6. **Fewer hairlines in the windows:** the skill ledger's per-row `border-top` and the Character tab's rules give way to spacing rhythm; a rule only between groups. Before: `before-win-character-1440x900.png`. Cheap.
  7. **Party board as portraits:** the party rows become portrait medallions (the bar's medal language, small) with their bars, no panel at all. Medium: needs portrait art (Asset Director).

- **2026-10-22, the old-client rewards and the Fox Companion are CUT (Tyler, approved: "do it all"; "we were supposed to delete all that"; lane/c-cuts-1022).** Cut: the quests (`QUEST_REWARDS`, `hr_claim_quest`, `hr_quest_rewards`), the collection milestones (`collection-milestones.js`, `hr_claim_milestone`, the loot roster; the staged ladders file keeps only the Bestiary total over `hr_hunt_roster`, renamed `2026-11-24-bestiary-roster.sql`), the renown rank claim (`hr_claim_rank`), the daily/weekly goals (`hr_claim_daily`, `hr_tally_state`, the daily-task set), the old seven-day login cycle (`rewards.js`, `hr_login_price`/`_catalogue`/`_streak`/`_bonus_ready`, `hr_claim_lookup`/`_last`), and the Fox Companion with the companion equip slot (item, shop row, doll slot; held foxes deleted and journalled, no refund). One drop file, `2026-12-02-old-rewards-cut.sql`. Kept: the premium login reward (`hr_login_claim`), the C2 refusal (`hr_login_claim_verify`, hr_apply calls it), every `ev:` counter, the Bestiary and collection reads. Rewards come back only as a new design (if the first hour should pay, a reward column on the hunt tutorial cards). `tests/old-rewards-gone.mjs` keeps them cut. Follow-ups: the old companion PET system (`COMPANIONS`, `hr_companion_equip`/`_grant`, four shop pet unlocks, the engine's companion XP) has no rebuilt-client screen and needs its own cut with an engine deploy; the engine still writes `kind='daily'` counters nothing reads.
- **2026-10-22, the second old-client cut: the engine's daily counters and three old claims (Tyler: "we were supposed to delete all that"; lane/c-cuts-1022b).** Cut: the engine's `kind='daily'` counters (`goal-period.js`, the `updateDaily` seam, the farm RPCs' daily rows, existing `ev:*` daily rows) and `raid_claim`, `world_event_claim`, `clan_board_claim` (+ `__ungated`, `raid_claims`), each a gold/gem faucet with no rebuilt-client caller. File: `2026-12-03-daily-claims-cut.sql`; hr-accrue redeploys with it. **Kept by Tyler the same day, as bases for later:** the companion pets ("I'll probably add those in later") and the premium daily login ("will be added later"); the Fox item and its equip slot stay cut (the pet system never used that slot: `hr_companion_equip` writes `player_state.companion_equipped`). Not ruled on, untouched: `raid_strike`, `world_event_*`, `clan_board_roll`/`_progress`, `clan_*`. **Premium login as a base (backend review):** sound: server clock and UTC day, once a day under the character lock plus a unique claim row, idempotent (`hr_intent_replay` + `player_intents`), premium checked at `now()`, one ledger row, reward from a generated server row, `login_days` only rises. Needs: a screen and a way to buy premium; a reward ladder keyed on `login_days` instead of one item; its own rate bucket (it borrows the cut `hr_claim_daily` one) and ledger kind (`quest` today); the dead C2 door (`hr_login_claim_verify`) out of `hr_apply` at its next restatement; a bag-capacity check (not verified). **Companion pets as a base (backend review):** server-authoritative ownership (unlock rows; shop pets priced and skill-gated by `hr_unlock_buy`) and equip (`hr_companion_equip`: ownership check, character lock, version bump, journalled; a same-pet re-equip is a no-op). Bonus parity holds by construction: `src/core/member.js` adds the companion layer for the edge settle and the browser view alike, draws no rng, data-driven from `COMPANIONS` with generated offers, grant allowlist and farm-yield rows. Weak: `hr_companion_grant` is client-callable and trusts the client's claimed source (only the allowlist and the whelp's egg gate it), so a redesign must grant pets from the server event (drop, skill, quest) instead; companion XP has no writer since lane C2 (every pet sits at level 1 while `hr_apply` keeps a dead `companion_xp` branch and cap); the starter fox is owned "by grammar" with no row; combat-stat pets and procs are omitted; the roster is an ad-hoc `companions` key inside `hr_state_of` rather than a state section; no rebuilt-client screen.

**2026-10-21: the four loot slots (L1), sample = Meadowbrook Thicket + the Sewer (lane/c-loot-loop).** Every monster drops: coin; ONE junk row (its own product, over 15%, sells, may also be a craft input: wolf meat cooks, pelts build rooms, bone chips feed Smithing and Prayer); bones only if it has bones (the Sump Slime lost its bones row); ONE imbuement item that no other monster drops (`src/data/imbuements.js`: uncommon odds, 5-15%, tier from the monster's Level: 1 below 30, 2 from 30, 3 from 80, provisional; book value 2 g per Level, raw, so the vendor bids a fifth of that); ONE equipment row (unchanged). Rares ride only on the carrier (Old Grayjaw: Alpha Fang; the Rat King: Crown Shard). The wolves give the physical kinds (Keen, Life Leech, Ward) and the Sewer gives the caster and element kinds (Mana Leech, Element Ward), so the two grounds differ in what they supply. **Imbuing is not built (L4).** Until it is, an imbuement item drops, stacks and trades, and the loot filter keeps it, because selling is opt-in per item and it is not `junk`. The vendor is its only use (VENDOR_TRASH, to shrink when L4 lands), and its text promises nothing. **Not in this sample:** the relic slot (it needs a relic item with a use, plus `relicMult`, which `member.js` already carries, applied in `hunt-rewards.js`; that is Systems' work), the Warrens and the Mill Cellar (`IMBUE_PENDING`, next batch), and the items' own art (each one uses the nearest creature part's icon, listed as a twin in `tests/pixel-icons.mjs`; handed to the Asset Director). H10 "one family per hunt" was superseded on 10-20 (a hunt may mix families), so the Sewer keeps its mix. Evidence: one seed, one hour, real settle. Thicket solo L5: coin 1,272 to 1,269 g/h, commons 601 to 591 g/h (Grayjaw's Dire Fang left; her pelt is now certain and the Briarcoat's is 30%, so the Thicket still nets more than the Cellar before it: forest-cave's 6-seed median is 1,802 g/h against 1,738), items 336 to 337 an hour (32 of them imbuement). Sewer party of four L8: coin 2,446 to 2,448 g/h per hero, commons 179 to 197, items 112 to 112 per hero an hour (8.8 of them imbuement). Healer +128% to +130%, Sewer solo floor +101% to +104%: every margin keeps the +25% minimum. **Gray Wolf coin [2,5] to [3,5] (lane b-supplies-bury): KEPT.** The starter bar eats at 60%, so the Thicket hero buys about twice the shrimp (store 80 to 164 g/h). With only the pelts above, the Thicket nets 1,714 g/h against the Cellar's 1,738 (forest-cave, 6-seed median); with the coin it nets 1,777.5. Coin is the lever because the bill is paid in coin and the Gray Wolf is the most-killed wolf. Merged numbers: Thicket solo coin 1,329, commons 591, store 164; Sewer party per hero coin 2,365, commons 201, Healer +140%, Sewer solo +71%.
- **2026-10-22, one setup = its own bar + its gear (designer, inside Tyler's 10-18 C4 "action sets (presets) need real save/name/rename/delete/switch UI"; CONFLICTS 2026-10-22 RULED).** Cause: the bar (action set) and Hunt prep's saved setups were two named lists, and no client path reached a second bar, so every setup shared one bar. Rulings: (1) one concept, the SETUP: a name, its own action bar (slots, conditions, target, stance, chase, distance, gold rule) and the gear it wears; 5 per hero; (2) the hero is always on one setup; the bar's plaque reads "Setup <name>" and edits that setup live (remembered, never re-saved); (3) Hunt prep: Use = wear its gear and switch to its bar; Save here / Save current setup = what you have on becomes that setup; both town-only; (4) Delete never takes the setup in use (marked "In use", no Delete); (5) rename on the plaque or in Hunt prep is the same name; (6) density stays on the hunt picker. Owed (Systems): density stored per hero per ground instead of in the set's options.
- **2026-10-22, the account depot's space and the storage chests (designer, inside Tyler's 10-22 "the depot is account-shared; its space grows by building storage chests in the house with gold"; for lane/c-account-shared).** **Base: 100 distinct stacks per account** (`DEPOT_BASE_STACKS`, unchanged). An active hero holds roughly 60-120 kinds by week one, so the depot fills when the gold ladder says chests arrive. **Ladder confirmed as proposed:** Millrace Crate +20 (1,500 g + 40 normal logs), Thicketwood Chest +30 (6,000 g + 30 oak planks + 10 copper bars), Ironbanded Strongbox +50 (25,000 g + 25 iron bars + 10 iron fittings), Stonecross Vault +100 (90,000 g + 30 steel bars + 15 timber beams). That is 75, 200, 500 and 900 g a stack, 300 stacks in all, below the ~340 kinds a player can obtain, so what to keep stays a decision. Every cost item is a real item with a source (grepped through `catalogue-coherence` itemSources). **Nobody is blocked (systems rule, for the lane):** a depot over its space never loses or locks anything. Withdrawing and adding to a stack it already holds always work; only a deposit that would open a NEW stack is refused, and the refusal names the next chest. A hunt's Depot loot rule then keeps the item in the bag; loot is never dropped. The refusal ships no earlier than the house window that can build a chest (§3.5: a limit whose only remedy cannot be built is half-built). Testers over 100 keep playing, selling and withdrawing; the EA wipe resets the rest.
- **2026-10-22, no ashlar (designer).** `ashlar` was cut with Stonemason on 10-14 (`stonecraft.js`), but `homestead-tiers.js` still asked for it on the Manor (6), Keep (12) and Castle (25). The server already charges what `src/data/shops.js` prices. No new stone item: a quarry, a skill and a recipe line to feed three purchases fail the purpose test. The ladder now matches the priced rows: Manor and Keep take iron fittings and timber beams (6 / 12 each), the Castle takes 6 keystones. `tools/gen-unlock-offers.mjs` now refuses a property cost that differs from its shop row or names a non-item. Mutant proven: ashlar back on the Castle turns `--check` red.
- **2026-10-22, every item has a door a player can open: the seed counter and the cut list (designer, inside Tyler's 10-19 "farming is in EA" and 10-20 "the Shop keeps gear and tools, no supplies"; lane/b-orphan-sources).** Problem: since `shop_buy` refuses unlisted rows (`not_listed`), eleven items had no source a player could reach, and `catalogue-coherence` still counted the refused rows as one. Six crop seeds (wheat, tomato, pumpkin, goldenroot, emberfruit, moonbloom) could never be planted. Carrot and potato seeds came only from quests, and no client claims quests. **Ruling (seeds):** the Shop lists a Seeds section, kind `seed`: every crop's seed, one per unit, at the counter's unit price. Turnip 5, carrot 10, wheat 15, potato 20, tomato 30 and pumpkin 50 sell at book value, so a first planting is never a gold decision. Goldenroot 150, emberfruit 300 and moonbloom 800 sell above book, a sink an endgame farmer meets. The nine legacy `seed.*` bundles are removed (one item, one price) and no vendor bid moves: the vendor pays at most half the cheapest unit, so buy-then-sell loses half. A seed is not a supply (Tyler named food, drink, ammo and runes). Farming level and plot tier still gate the planting, never the purchase. Rows: `src/data/supply-basics.js`; server: `2026-11-21-alpha-catalogue.generated.sql` + `2026-11-21-shop-seeds.sql` (STAGED). Not chosen: harvest returns seeds (an SQL rewrite of `hr_farm_harvest` for a decision the farm window should make: see backlog) and seed drops (every walked monster already carries its one equipment drop, Tyler 10-14). **Ruling (the rest): cut.** Apprentice Staff, Apprentice Wand, Apprentice Primer and Stone Maul copy the bound practice staff, wand, spellbook and club stat for stat. Every hero already holds those from the kit, so a bought or dropped copy adds no decision (purpose test). Merge rung 1 of those four ladders into the practice kit (systems-engineer, backlog). The Fox Companion (+2 Melee strength in a companion slot nothing else fills, Defense 15) is a stat pet in a game whose looks are cosmetic. The designer recommends the cut, for Tyler to approve. Until then the five sit on `catalogue-coherence`'s shrink-only `PENDING_SOURCE` list, each with its owner. **Guard:** a shop row is a source only if a Shop tab lists it (`SHOP_LISTED_OFFER_IDS`, one definition, read by `shop_buy`, `catalogue-coherence` and the bag tooltip's "Sold at the shop"). Mutants: an item whose only door is a hidden row, the seed counter closing, and a stale pending row are all caught.
  **Walk-through (a new hero's first crop, real data).** (1) Create a hero: bag holds Turnip Seed x8, 500 g (`start-kit.js` START_INVENTORY, START_CURRENCY; farmPlots 4). (2) Open the farm: **BLOCKED, no client door.** No window, station or intent in `src/` calls `hr_farm_plant`, `hr_farm_harvest`, `hr_farm_water` or `hr_farm_upgrade_plot` (the server RPCs exist). Today a new hero cannot plant at all, in any hour; this, not seeds, is why farming sits at zero. Once the farm window ships: (3) plant turnip on plots 1-4 at minute 1 (plot tier 1 unlocks turnip, `farm.js` PLOT_TIERS[1]; turnip needs Farming 1, `gathering.js` CROPS). (4) Harvest at 4 h, or about 2 h if watered at once (growth = elapsed + min(water bonus, elapsed), 2 growth-hours per hour for 2 h): 2-4 turnips and 336 Farming XP a plot. (5) Replant from the 4 seeds left, then Shop > Seeds > Turnip Seed at 5 g (this lane). (6) Carrots need Farming 10 and plot tier 2 (500 g at Farming 5, `farm.js` PLOT_TIER_PRICES) and cost 10 g a seed. **Release note:** the Seeds list is a purchase with no use until the farm window lands (§3.5: half-built stays hidden). Ship this lane with the farm window, or right before it.
- **2026-10-22, what gold buys in the first hours: the Shop's outfitter (designer, inside Tyler's 10-20 "the Shop keeps gear and tools" and 10-18 "Defense trains only in armour"; lane/b-gold-sinks).** Problem (play report #5): 5,153 gp at 2.5 h with only an axe and a pickaxe to buy, and Defense at 0 XP after 117 kills because a new hero wears no armour. **Ruling:** the Shop sells the small pieces no walked ground drops, tier 1 and Level 1 only, at 2 x book value. That is the tools' rule: the vendor pays at most half back, so buy-then-sell loses. Monsters keep the helmets, bodies, legs, boots and weapons (Tyler 10-20: gear drops are a real slot), so the Shop never competes with a drop. Gloves and belts are armour, so the first purchase starts Defense. Rows (`src/data/supply-basics.js`, kind `gear`): Leather Gloves 160 (medium: neither health nor mana, right for every way to fight), Bronze Belt 220 (heavy: more health, a little less magic and ranged accuracy, the shield hero's pick), Traveler Cape 300, Copper Studs 120, Copper Ring 240 (two ring slots), Hunter Necklace 360 (ring and necklace are Melee stats). A full Melee set costs 1,640 g; any other hero's useful set (gloves, cape, earrings) costs 580 g. The light and the other tier-1 gloves and belts are not sold: they are field-salvage finds, and a salvage piece is never shop stock (`tests/field-salvage.mjs` (a); the generator refuses it). The five legacy `equip.*` rows for these items are removed, and `shop_buy` now refuses any id no Shop tab lists (`not_listed`: a price no window shows is still a price a forged intent can hit); the other legacy seed and equip rows stay only as the vendor price list. No starter armour in the kit (cut 10-21, still cut): the Thicket is survivable without it, and 380 of the 500 starting gold now buys gloves and a belt. While Defense XP is 0, "What next" says "Your Defense is not training: it rises only while you wear armour. The Shop sells gloves and belts." Help gains "What gold is for" and "Armour and Defense".
  **Gold ladder.** Measured from the play report's server numbers and the real settle (`tests/hunt-core/fixtures/play-ground.mjs`, one seed). At 0 min the hero holds 500 g: gloves and belt, 380 g. At 25 min, about 814 g: cape and earrings. At about 1 h, 2,000 g (the Thicket nets 1,650-3,150 g/h per role, outfitted or not): rings and necklace for Melee, and the second hero's own gloves from its own 500 g. At 2.5 h, about 5,000 g: saving for the third hero slot (15,000 g, about 7 h). After about 8 h of hunting: a stamina refill (400 + 100 x Level for 4 h, 1,600 g at Level 12, rising with each buy). At Level 30: Blessings (100/150/200 g + per Level). In week one: storage chests (lane/c-account-shared proposal: 1,500 g + 40 logs, then 6,000, then 25,000; ruled as proposed, they arrive when the depot fills), bestiary stage fees, and slots 4 and 5 (40,000 / 120,000).
  **Played on the real settle (start kit, Thicket L5, one hour, seed 1):** Melee in Leather Gloves + Bronze Belt: Defense XP 0 → 3,970, 0 deaths, net 2,018 → 2,561 g. In Leather Gloves only: Ranger 0 → 681, Mage 0 → 334, Healer 0 → 806 Defense XP/h. Every margin over the store bill stays far above the +25% minimum (Thicket +265% to +2,637%). The Sewer party is untouched: the pieces only add defence or damage.

  Not done: browser play (the server half is STAGED: apply both 2026-11-20 files, then redeploy the edge, then ship the client). Handoffs: "Equip upgrades" in one press (four separate Equip menus today: first principle) to Systems + Art Director; the Shop's Gear list needs an art pass (visual gate).
- **2026-10-22, every room does one thing (designer, inside "home shared per account" 10-15, "housing at EA: benches, depot, trophy shelf" 10-14/15 and Master Design Reference §9 "a home never grants XP or a resource advantage").** The Garden, Library and Trophy Room paid nothing, so the House window offered nothing for them. Purpose test passed by each, no cuts: (1) **Library = the runecrafting bench**: rune binding 2/4/6/8/10% faster (`runeSpeed`, governed under the 20% permanent cap); runecrafting leaves the Workshop, which keeps crafting and stonemason. It plugs into the Mage/Healer supply loop (runes are hunt supplies) on the same grammar as Kitchen/Forge/Shrine. Library → skill XP was REJECTED: §9 forbids home XP. (2) **Garden = farm beds**: +1/2/3/4/5 beds for every character of the account on top of the property's (tier+1)×2 (`farmPlots`, server cap `hr_farm_plot_cap`, top 17 under the 64 fuse); capacity bought with gold, as rulings v2 §2 allows; no yield or growth rate. (3) **Trophy Room = the trophy wall**: 2/3/4/5/6 mounts that hang the best claimed bestiary trophies in the home (`trophyMounts`, display only, no power). Data `src/data/perks.js`; server `2026-11-23-room-purpose-room-perks.generated.sql` + `2026-11-23-garden-beds.sql` (STAGED); guard `tests/room-purpose.mjs`. **Reopen dependencies (the purchase is real, the payoff is not yet reachable from the new client):** the Garden needs the farm window (Farming is IN at EA, 10-19); the Trophy Room needs the Bestiary window's trophy claim and the home scene reading rooms/trophies from the session instead of the old `HearthriseRooms`/`HearthriseTrophies` globals. If either is not in by the reopen, the House window must hide that room again.

- **2026-10-22, a source must be one a player can reach (designer; lane/c-unreachable-items).** Cause: `catalogue-coherence` C1 counted any drop as a source, and 140 items drop only from monsters no open ground spawns (the old roster); 52 more sit in a shop table the Shop does not list or are crafted from such inputs. Only 167 of 517 items are reachable today (158 more are sourceless, held in AWAITING_ROSTER). Rulings: (1) a REACHABLE source is a monster on an open ground, the listed Shop table (tools), the start kit, gathering, a server quest reward, a coded grant, or a crop or craft whose every input is reachable (`reachableSources()`); (2) every other item carries a fate in `UNREACHABLE` (`src/data/catalogue-coherence.js`), and C11 (C9 until the v0.2.30 merge renumbered it) proves each planned fate by opening its door: staged 20 (Mosslight opens: its drops plus what Magic Essence feeds, the rune bar line and deep runes), farming 22 (a seed counter: six seeds, the five crops after potato, and the crafted drinks past Carrot Juice), premium 1, shop_gear 11 (b: the starter counter the edge already sells and the Shop window does not list), greenhaven 49 (tier 1-3 gear and region materials for the next grounds), later 72 (tier 4+ rungs, old-roster uniques, dragon/undead/demon/void materials and their late recipes: Tyler keeps or cuts), cut 17 (vendor trash of homeless creatures: Tyler approves); (3) the list only shrinks: rows outside `staged` sit under `UNREACHABLE_CEILING` (172). **Sourced in this lane: none.** Every recipe the reachable game is blocked from opens at skill 25 or higher, and its root is a seed (no farm door), Mosslight's essence (staged), or a creature with no Greenhaven home. The four-slot ruling gives each open-ground monster one equipment row, and the Cellar's Giant Rat and Slime are fixture-pinned, so no open drop table has room without a new ground. **Found:** no reachable item fills gloves, belt, necklace, ring, earrings, cape or companion; the cloth line (Mage, Healer) has no reachable piece; the wand, staff, spellbook, axes, club and crossbow never upgrade past the practice kit.
- **2026-10-22, the style ladder: every way to fight climbs (designer; lane/c-style-gear).** Cause: on the open grounds only the sword climbed (Iron Sword from the Cistern Spider, Steel Sword from the Goblin Skulker). The wand, spellbook, staff, bow, crossbow, hatchets and club never left the practice kit (the Shortbow and Bronze Sword copy the practice bow and sword stat for stat), and no cloth armour could be had. Four of six styles had no gear chase. **Ruling:** (1) every style climbs at the sword's rungs: rung 0 the practice kit at Level 1, rung 1 tier 2 worn at skill 15 (the Sewer's band), rung 2 tier 3 at skill 30 (the Warrens floor). (2) The open grounds' equipment rows are full (one per monster, five slots per ground), so the other styles' rungs are MADE at the Work window from wood or metal the hero gathers plus a material the Sewer or the Cellar drops; the hunt feeds the bench. The sword keeps its drop. (3) Cloth is the caster's line from Level 1: Apprentice (Defense 1) sewn from the Hive Wasp's and Cistern Spider's Silk Thread, Adept (Defense 15) lined with the Culvert Bat's wing. (4) A made piece never sells for more than the book value of its inputs (`tests/style-gear.mjs` SG-4), so the bench is never a gold faucet; the Longbow, Oak Staff and Iron Warhammer fell from their legacy 400 / 300 / 550 g to their ladder values (135 / 130 / 145). (5) The club ladder is two-handed, like the practice club (Tyler 10-16: two-handed club = crush). **Rows (17, sample):** 2 new items, Iron Hatchets (axe, two-handed, 7/7) and Oak Crossbow (8/7: a little less accuracy and more damage than the Longbow's 9/6), as `gear-tiers.js` rungs whose tier 1 is the practice weapon; 15 recipes in `src/data/style-gear.js`: Forge Iron Hatchets (2 iron bar + 1 normal plank) and Forge Iron Warhammer (3 + 2) at Smithing 15; Carve Oak Wand (2 oak plank + 1 silk), Bind Oakboard Spellbook (2 oak plank + 4 bat wing), Carve Oak Staff (3 oak plank + 3 bone chips), String Longbow (3 oak plank + 2 silk), String Oak Crossbow (3 oak plank + 1 silk) at Crafting 15; Apprentice Slippers / Hat / Robe Bottom / Robe Top (1 / 1 / 2 / 3 silk) at Crafting 1 / 3 / 6 / 9; Adept Slippers / Hat / Robe Bottom / Robe Top (silk + bat wing) at Crafting 15 / 17 / 19 / 22. Work-window tabs Weapons (Smithing, Crafting) and Armour (Crafting). Server: `2026-11-30-gear-ladder-catalogue.generated.sql` then `2026-11-30-hunt-sessions-catalogue.generated.sql` (STAGED), and the hr-accrue edge (recipes.js) deploys with them. The unreachable ceiling falls 172 -> 140 at the merge (seed counter and outfitter closed two fates) -> 134 (six greenhaven rows made; the Longbow left `staged`). **Depends on lane/b-town-skills** (the Work window is the only client door to Woodcutting, Mining, Smithing and Crafting; until it ships the recipes are a server capability with no button, §3.5: ship together).
  **Played on the real settle (`play-ground.mjs`, two seeds, one hour, per hero).** Solo at Level 15 in the Sewer, practice weapon -> rung 1, kills/h and net g/h: sword 268 -> 286 (+7%), net 2,243 -> 2,631; hatchets 408 -> 423 (+4%), 4,371 -> 4,960; club 436 -> 457 (+5%), 5,056 -> 6,093; bow 280 -> 305 (+9%), 3,022 -> 3,363; crossbow 282 -> 311 (+10%), 2,658 -> 3,570; wand + book 313 -> 355 (+13%), 3,684 -> 4,830; staff 250 -> 281 (+12%), 3,005 -> 3,895. 0 deaths in every run; every margin +58% or more (the +25% minimum holds). In the Thicket at 15 every style is spawn-bound (578-648 kills/h either way). Armour at Level 10 in the Sewer, bare -> its line: tank + 3 bronze plate (Sewer drops) net -172 -> 2,411, 2 deaths -> 0; ranger + 4 leather (Thicket drops) 1,661 -> 3,006; Mage + Apprentice cloth 3,318 -> 4,940; Healer + Apprentice 2,364 -> 3,872. At 15, Mage rung + Adept 4,830 -> 6,587 and Healer 3,895 -> 5,174 (cloth buys magic damage and Ward, so the store bill falls by two thirds). Sewer party of four at 15 (sword, bow, wand + book, staff): practice 385 kills/h, net 3,119 -> rungs + Adept 481, 4,023; at 20: 415 -> 494, 3,414 -> 4,361. **Time to first upgrade** (fresh hero, every skill 1, PACE x1.6 action time, x0.39 XP, no tool): the sword's Iron Sword drops at about 0.8 to 1.6 an hour of Sewer (`drop-tables` 0.79/h solo at 8; one seed at 15 gave 3); the Mage's, Healer's and Ranger's tier-2 weapons take 1.5 h of Work (Woodcutting 1 -> 15 on normal trees 33 min, 206 more logs 16 min, Crafting 1 -> 15 sawing them 40 min, then 2-3 oak logs and planks) plus 1-4 silk, bat wings or bone chips (Sewer: 3-5 silk, 19-25 bat wings, 8-12 bone chips an hour); hatchets and club 1.2 h (Mining 24 min + 325 more ore 26 min, Smithing 21 min on bronze bars, then 2-3 iron ore and bars, a normal plank). Apprentice cloth: Crafting 9 is 17 min of sawing, and 7 silk is 1.2-2.3 h of the Cellar (Hive Wasp, 3-6 silk an hour) or about 1.5 h of the Sewer. **Known cost, ruled acceptable:** a made style spends ~1.2-1.5 h of a hero's time once, which a sword hero spends hunting; the Work also levels four skills that feed arrows, whetstones, tools and the house, the next rung (Crafting / Smithing 30) builds on it, and with up to four heroes playing at once (Tyler 10-17) a second hero can work while the first hunts (the depot is account-shared). Missing art: Iron Hatchets and Oak Crossbow wear their practice pair's icon (`tests/pixel-icons.mjs` KNOWN_TWINS), handed to the Asset Director.
  **Next batch (rung 2, skill 30, the Warrens floor; ≤20 rows):** Steel Hatchets and Steel Warhammer (Smithing 30, steel bars from the Warrens' iron ore and coal); Willow Wand, Willowleaf Spellbook, Willow Staff, Willow Longbow and Willow Crossbow (Crafting 30, willow planks + a Warrens material: goblin ear or totem); Scholar cloth helmet / body / legs / boots (Defense 30); the tier-1 and tier-2 cloth gloves and sash (field-salvage today); studded leather (tier 2) for the Ranger, whose only tier-2 pieces are Mosslight drops. Then cut the Shortbow and Bronze Sword copies (board backlog, systems).
